So when  KNP Logistics Group  (formerly Knights of Old) celebrated more than a century and a half of operations, it had mastered the art of survival. For 158 years, KNP adapted and endured, building a transport business that operated 500 trucks across the UK. But in June 2025, one easily guessed password brought down the company in a matter of days.  The Northamptonshire-based firm  fell victim  to the Akira ransomware group after hackers gained access by guessing an employee's weak password.
